# Закон за ратификација на измените и дополнувањата на Договорот за слободна трговија меѓу Република Македонија и ЕФТА државите

> **This is the law as enacted. Amendments are not incorporated below — each one is
> a separate file in this repository and a commit in this file's history.**

##### Член 1

Се ратификуваат измените и дополнувањата на Договорот за слободна трговија меѓу Република Северна Македонија и ЕФТА државите содржани во Одлуката на ЕФТА- Северна Македонија на Мешовитиот комитет бр.1 од 2021 година, потпишана во Женева на 14 јули 2021 година и во Скопје на 23 јули 2021 година.

##### Член 2

Измените и дополнувањата на Договорот од членот 1 од овој закон содржани во Одлуката на ЕФТА-Северна Македонија на Мешовитиот комитет бр.1 од 2021 година, во оригинал на англиски и во превод на македонски јазик, гласат: . No.10f2021

## DEFINITION OF THE CONCEPT QF “ORIGINATING PRODUCTS” AND METHODS OF ADMINISTRATIVE COOPERATION THE EFTA-NORTH MACEDONIA JOINT COMMITTEE,

Recalling the Parties’ intention to participate actively in the process of economic integration in the Buro-Mediterranean region, and expressing their readiness to cooperate in seeking ways and means to strengthen this process; .

Recalling that the Pan-Euro-Mediterranean system of cumulation of origin established by the European Union, the EFTA States, the People's Democratic Republic of Algeria, the Arab Republic of Egypt, the State of Israel, the Hashemite Kingdom of Jordan, the Republic of Lebanon, the Kingdom of Morocco, the Syrian Arab Republic, the Republic of Tunisia, the PLO for the benefit of the Palestinian Authority of the West Bank and the Gaza Strip, the Republic of Turkey and the Faroe Islands, consists of a network of Free Trade Agreements that set out identical rules of origin allowing for diagonal cumulation of origin among the member countries of the system;

Noting that the Regional Convention on Pan-Euro-Mediterranean Preferential Rules of Origin (hereinafter referred to as the “Convention™) aims at replacing the.protocols.on rules of origin currently in force among the member countries of the Pan-Euro-Meditérranean system of cumtilation of origin;

Considering that the Republic of Albania, Bosnia and Herzegovina, North Macedonia, Montenegro, the Republic of Serbia, Kosovo (urider Resolution 1244(1999) of the United Nations Security Council), as well as the Republic of Moldova, Georgia and Ukaine are Contracting Parties of the Convention and that the Pan-Furo-Mediterranean system is thus extended to these countries;

Noting that the Convention entered into force in relation to Liechtenstein, Norway and Switzerland on 1 January 2012, to Iceland on 1% May 2012 and to North Macedonia on 1% August 2012;

2 04250 Considering that the Convention shall not lead to auy less favourable situation than previously under the Free rade Agreement between the EFTA States snd The Republic of Macedonia (hercinafier referred to as the “Agreement”);

Taving regard to Article 33 of the Agreement, empowenng the Joint Committee o ainend the Annexes and Protocols to the Agieanent, DECIDES that:

1. The text of Protocol B to the Agreement, concerning the definidons of the concept of “originating products” and methods of admunmistrative cooperation shall-be replaced by the (ext int the Annex to this Decision.

2. This Decision shall enter into force on the first day of the second month after the last Party has notified the Depositary of the completion of its internal requirements.

3. The Secretary-General of the European Free Trade Association shall deposit the text of this Decision with the Depositary.

3 04250 DONE at Geneva and Skopje in one original. For Teeland For the Republic of Noth Macedonia For the Principality of Liechtenstein Ath. July 2624 {date and signature)

For the Kingdom of Norway pf. Tuly 822 For the Swiss Confederation 4 04250 ANNEX, TO DECISION Ne. 1 OF 2021

## OF TIE EFTA NORT[I MACEDONIA JOINT COMMITTELR PROTOCOL B CONCERNING THE DEFINITION OF THE CONCEPT OF “ORIGINATING PRODUCTS? AND MIETHODS OF ADMINISTRATIVE COOPERATION

Article 1 - Applicable Rules of Origin 1 For the purpose of implementing this agreement, Appendix I and the relevant provisions of Appendix II to the Regional Convention on pan-Euro-Mediterranean preferential rules of origin (‘the Convention’), as it may be subsequently amended, shall apply and are hereby incorporated into and made part of the Agreement, mutatis mutandis.

2. All references to the ‘relevant agreement’ in Appendix I and in the relevant provisions of Appendix 11 to the Convention shall be construed so as to mean this Agreement.

##### Article 2

Alternative Applicable Rules of Origin

1. Notwithstanding Article 1, for the purpose of implementing this Agreement, products which acquire preferential origin in accordance with the provisions set out in Appendix A to this Protocol shall also be considered as originating in an EFTA State or in North Macedonia.

2. The alternative rules shall apply until the amendment of the Convention enters into force. Alice 3 Full Gumulation

1. Working or processing carried out in a Party to the Central European Free Trade Agreement (hereinafter referred to as the “CEFTA Party/Parties” shall be considered as having been carried out in an EFTA State when the products obtained undergo subsequent working or processing in an EFTA State. Where pursuant to this provision the originating products are obtained in two or more of the Parties concerned, they shall be considered as originating in an EFTA State only if the working or processing goes beyond (he provisions In the Convention ur in the dlenative rules concerning insufficient working or processing.

2. Working or processing carried out in an EFTA State shall be considered as having been carried out in 2 CEFTA Party when the products obtained undergo subsequent working or processing in a CEFTA Party. Where pursuant to this provision the originating products are obtained in two or more of the Parties concerned, they shall be considered as originating in a CEFTA. Party only if 5 04250 the working or processing goes beyond the provisions iin Convention or in the alternative rules concettilng insufficient wotldng or processing.

3. The cumulation provided for in this Article may only be applied provided that a preferential trade agreement in accordance with Article XXIV of the General Agreetnent ou Tariffs and Trade (GATT) is applicable between the countries and territories involved in the acquisition of rhe originating status and the country or territory of destination.

##### Article 4

Prohibition of drawback of, or of exemption from, customs duties The prohibition in Appendix 1 to the Convention and in the alternative rules, of drawback of, ar exemption from, customs duties, shall not apply in bilateral trade between the Parties to this Agreement.

##### Article 5

Dispute Settlement

1. Article 31 of the Agreement shall apply with respect to the settlement of any disputes concerning the interpretation or application of Appendix I and the relevant provisions of Appendix II to the Convention, including their Annexes and of the alternative rules.

2. In all cases the settlement of disputes between the importer and the customs authorities of the importing country shall take place under the legislation of that country

##### Article 8

Withdrawal from the Convention

1. Should one of the Parties give notice in writing to the depositary of the Convention of their intention to withdraw from the Convention according to Article 9 thereof, the Parties shall immediately enter into negotiations on rules of origin for the purpose of implementing this Agreement.

2. Until the entry Into force of such newly negotiated rules of origin, the rules of origin contained in Appendix and, where appropriate, the relevant provisions of Appendix Il to the Convention, applicable at the moment of withdrawal, shall continue to apply and the alternative rules may continue to apply to this Agreement. However, as of the moment of withdrawal, the rules of origin contained in Appendix I and, where appropriate, the relevant provisions of Appendix If to the Convention and in the alternative rules shall be construed so as to allow bilateral camulation only between the withdrawing Party and each of the other Parties.

##### Article 7

Electronically issued moveniernt certificates EUR.

1. As an alternative to the provisions regarding the issuance of movement certificates, the Parties shall accept electronically issued movement certificates EUR.1. Considering the digitalised system to issue movement certificates EUR.1, the formal requirements of electronically issued . 6 04 250 movement certificates EUR.1 are stated in Paragraph 3. The customs authorities of the exporting and the importing Party may agree on other formal requirements of electronically issued movement certificates EUR. 1.

2. Each exporting Patty shiall infonn the EFTA Secretaviat about the readiness of the issuance of electronic movement certificates EUR.1 and all technical issues related to such implementation (issuance, submission and verification of an electronic certificate).

3.1f agreed by the customs authorities of the exporting and importing Parties, paragraphs 1 and 2 of Annex Ila to the Convention shall not apply if the movement certificate is issued and validated electronically, and the following applies:

(a) luk stanps used by the customs ol competent governnental authmities for the validation of the movement certificate KUR.1 (Box 11) may be replaced with an image or electronic stamps;

(b) Boxes Ll and 12 may contain facsimile or electronic signatures instead of original signatures;

(c) the information in Box 11 concerning the form and number of the export document shall be indicated only where requested by the regulations of the exporting Party; * (d). it shall bear a serial number or a code by which it can be identified; and (e) it may be issued in any of the official languages of the Parties or in English.

##### Article 8

Transitional provisions - cumulation Until the application of tie revised rules of the Convention and notwithstanding paragraph S of Article 16 and paragraph 3 of Article 21 of Appendix I to the Convention, where cumulation involves only EFTA States, the Faroe Islands, the EU, Turkey, the participants in the Stabilisation and Association Process, Moldova, Georgia and Ukraine the proof of origin may be 2 movement certificate EUR.1 or an origin declaration.

Article 9:

Sub-Committee on Customs and Origin Matters

1. The Parties shall: maintain the Sub-Committee on Customs and Origin Matters (héreinafter referred to as the “Sub-Committee”), established by Joint Committee Decision 2/2003.

2. The functions of the Sub-Committee shall be to exchange information, review developments, prepare and co-ordinate positions on rules of origin and assist the Joint Cormmittee regarding: (a) rules of origin and administrative co-operation as set out in the Convention; {b) other matters that are referred to the Sub-Committee by the Joint Committee. 7 04250

3. The Sub-Committee shall be chaired alternatively by a representative of an EFTA State or Nuit Macedouls fur an agreed period of Lime. The Jsahiperson shall be elected at the first meeting of the Sub-Committee. The Sub-Committee shall act by consensus.

4. The Sub-Committee shall report tv the Joint Committee, The Sub-Comunittee ay make recommendations to the Joint Committee on matters related to its functions.

5. The Sub-Cotmuittee shell weet as often as required. It may be convened by the Joint Committee or upon request of any Party. The venue shall alternate between North Macedonia and an EFTA State.

6. A provisional agenda for each meeting shall be prepared by the chairperson in consultation with all Parties, and forwarded ta the Parties, as a general rule, not later than two weeks before the meeting.
